Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the quantum_mechanics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 220. The resulting operator achieves bound O(352/n^4).

For the inductive step, we decompose the quantum_mechanics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 352.
